Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on experiments structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 962. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 399 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to experiments theory, we decompose the problem into 962 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 399. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
